  1 | """
  2 | Test script for AI Medical Chatbot
  3 | Tests various scenarios including safe queries, unsafe queries, and edge cases
  4 | """
  5 | 
  6 | import requests
  7 | import json
  8 | from datetime import datetime
  9 | 
 10 | # Configuration
 11 | BASE_URL = "http://localhost:5000"
 12 | CHATBOT_API = f"{BASE_URL}/api/chatbot"
 13 | 
 14 | # Test session ID
 15 | SESSION_ID = f"test_session_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"
 16 | 
 17 | # Colors for terminal output
 18 | class Colors:
 19 |     GREEN = '\033[92m'
 20 |     RED = '\033[91m'
 21 |     YELLOW = '\033[93m'
 22 |     BLUE = '\033[94m'
 23 |     RESET = '\033[0m'
 24 |     BOLD = '\033[1m'
 25 | 
 26 | def print_header(text):
 27 |     """Print a formatted header"""
 28 |     print(f"\n{Colors.BOLD}{Colors.BLUE}{'='*70}{Colors.RESET}")
 29 |     print(f"{Colors.BOLD}{Colors.BLUE}{text.center(70)}{Colors.RESET}")
 30 |     print(f"{Colors.BOLD}{Colors.BLUE}{'='*70}{Colors.RESET}\n")
 31 | 
 32 | def print_test(test_name, expected_behavior):
 33 |     """Print test information"""
 34 |     print(f"{Colors.BOLD}Test: {test_name}{Colors.RESET}")
 35 |     print(f"Expected: {expected_behavior}")
 36 |     print(f"{'-'*70}")
 37 | 
 38 | def print_result(success, message=""):
 39 |     """Print test result"""
 40 |     if success:
 41 |         print(f"{Colors.GREEN}✓ PASS{Colors.RESET} {message}\n")
 42 |     else:
 43 |         print(f"{Colors.RED}✗ FAIL{Colors.RESET} {message}\n")
 44 | 
 45 | def send_query(query):
 46 |     """Send a query to the chatbot"""
 47 |     try:
 48 |         response = requests.post(
 49 |             f"{CHATBOT_API}/query",
 50 |             json={
 51 |                 "query": query,
 52 |                 "session_id": SESSION_ID
 53 |             },
 54 |             timeout=30
 55 |         )
 56 |         return response.json()
 57 |     except Exception as e:
 58 |         return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
 59 | 
 60 | def check_status():
 61 |     """Check chatbot status"""
 62 |     try:
 63 |         response = requests.get(f"{CHATBOT_API}/status", timeout=5)
 64 |         return response.json()
 65 |     except Exception as e:
 66 |         return {"available": False, "message": str(e)}
 67 | 
 68 | def clear_conversation():
 69 |     """Clear conversation history"""
 70 |     try:
 71 |         response = requests.post(
 72 |             f"{CHATBOT_API}/clear",
 73 |             json={"session_id": SESSION_ID},
 74 |             timeout=5
 75 |         )
 76 |         return response.json()
 77 |     except Exception as e:
 78 |         return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
 79 | 
 80 | # Test Cases
 81 | def test_status():
 82 |     """Test 1: Check chatbot status"""
 83 |     print_header("TEST 1: CHATBOT STATUS")
 84 |     print_test("Chatbot Status Check", "Service should be available")
 85 |     
 86 |     status = check_status()
 87 |     
 88 |     if status.get('available'):
 89 |         mode = status.get('mode', 'Unknown')
 90 |         print_result(True, f"Chatbot is available in {mode} mode")
 91 |     else:
 92 |         print_result(False, f"Chatbot unavailable: {status.get('message')}")
 93 |     
 94 |     return status.get('available', False)
 95 | 
 96 | def test_medication_query():
 97 |     """Test 2: Medication information query"""
 98 |     print_header("TEST 2: MEDICATION QUERY (SAFE)")
 99 |     print_test(
100 |         "Medication Information",
101 |         "Should provide medication details from patient records"
102 |     )
103 |     
104 |     query = "What medications am I taking?"
105 |     print(f"Query: {query}\n")
106 |     
107 |     result = send_query(query)
108 |     
109 |     if result.get('success'):
110 |         response = result.get('response', '')
111 |         print(f"Response:\n{response}\n")
112 |         
113 |         # Check if response contains medication information
114 |         has_medication_info = any(word in response.lower() for word in ['medication', 'prescription', 'dosage'])
115 |         print_result(has_medication_info, "Response contains medication information")
116 |     else:
117 |         print_result(False, f"Error: {result.get('message', 'Unknown error')}")
118 | 
119 | def test_dietary_advice():
120 |     """Test 3: Dietary advice query"""
121 |     print_header("TEST 3: DIETARY ADVICE (SAFE)")
122 |     print_test(
123 |         "Dietary Recommendations",
124 |         "Should provide diagnosis-specific dietary advice"
125 |     )
126 |     
127 |     query = "What should I eat with my condition?"
128 |     print(f"Query: {query}\n")
129 |     
130 |     result = send_query(query)
131 |     
132 |     if result.get('success'):
133 |         response = result.get('response', '')
134 |         print(f"Response:\n{response}\n")
135 |         
136 |         # Check if response contains dietary information
137 |         has_dietary_info = any(word in response.lower() for word in ['food', 'eat', 'diet', 'recommended'])
138 |         print_result(has_dietary_info, "Response contains dietary advice")
139 |     else:
140 |         print_result(False, f"Error: {result.get('message', 'Unknown error')}")
141 | 
142 | def test_allergy_query():
143 |     """Test 4: Allergy information query"""
144 |     print_header("TEST 4: ALLERGY INFORMATION (SAFE)")
145 |     print_test(
146 |         "Allergy Information",
147 |         "Should list patient's known allergies"
148 |     )
149 |     
150 |     query = "What are my allergies?"
151 |     print(f"Query: {query}\n")
152 |     
153 |     result = send_query(query)
154 |     
155 |     if result.get('success'):
156 |         response = result.get('response', '')
157 |         print(f"Response:\n{response}\n")
158 |         
159 |         # Check if response contains allergy information
160 |         has_allergy_info = 'allerg' in response.lower()
161 |         print_result(has_allergy_info, "Response contains allergy information")
162 |     else:
163 |         print_result(False, f"Error: {result.get('message', 'Unknown error')}")
164 | 
165 | def test_appointment_query():
166 |     """Test 5: Appointment scheduling query"""
167 |     print_header("TEST 5: APPOINTMENT SCHEDULING (SAFE)")
168 |     print_test(
169 |         "Appointment Booking",
170 |         "Should guide user to appointment booking"
171 |     )
172 |

# =========================
# CHATBOT API ENDPOINTS
# =========================

# Import chatbot service
from chatbot.service import get_chatbot_service

@app.route('/api/chatbot/query', methods=['POST'])
def chatbot_query():
    """Process chatbot query"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        query = data.get('query', '')
        session_id = data.get('session_id')
        
        if not query:
            return jsonify({
                'success': False,
                'message': 'Query is required'
            }), 400
        
        # Get chatbot service
        chatbot = get_chatbot_service()
        
        # Process query with current patient ID
        result = chatbot.process_query(
            patient_id=CURRENT_PATIENT_ID,
            query=query,
            session_id=session_id
        )
        
        return jsonify(result)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            'success': False,
            'error': str(e),
            'message': 'An error occurred processing your query'
        }), 500


@app.route('/api/chatbot/status', methods=['GET'])
def chatbot_status():
    """Check chatbot service status"""
    try:
        chatbot = get_chatbot_service()
        
        return jsonify({
            'available': True,
            'message': 'Chatbot service is ready',
            'huggingface_available': chatbot.use_huggingface,
            'mode': 'LLM' if chatbot.use_huggingface else 'Template'
        })
        
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            'available': False,
            'message': f'Chatbot service unavailable: {str(e)}'
        }), 503


@app.route('/api/chatbot/history', methods=['GET'])
def chatbot_history():
    """Get conversation history"""
    try:
        session_id = request.args.get('session_id')
        
        if not session_id:
            return jsonify({
                'success': False,
                'message': 'Session ID is required'
            }), 400
        
        chatbot = get_chatbot_service()
        history = chatbot.get_conversation_history(session_id)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'history': history,
            'session_id': session_id
        })
        
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            'success': False,
            'error': str(e)
        }), 500


@app.route('/api/chatbot/clear', methods=['POST'])
def chatbot_clear():
    """Clear conversation history"""
    try:
        data = request.get_json()
        session_id = data.get('session_id')
        
        if not session_id:
            return jsonify({
                'success': False,
                'message': 'Session ID is required'
            }), 400
        
        chatbot = get_chatbot_service()
        chatbot.clear_conversation_history(session_id)
        
        return jsonify({
            'success': True,
            'message': 'Conversation history cleared'
        })
        
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            'success': False,
            'error': str(e)
        }), 500

</content>

## ✅ Complete Implementation Summary

### 1. **Backend - HuggingFace Integration**
- ✅ Updated `chatbot/config.py` with HuggingFace settings
- ✅ Refactored `chatbot/service.py` to use HuggingFace Serverless Inference API
- ✅ Added `huggingface_hub` to requirements.txt
- ✅ Implemented proper error handling, rate limiting, and retry logic
- ✅ Maintained template-based fallback mode

### 2. **Frontend - Chatbot Interface**
- ✅ Added chatbot card to LEFT column (first item above "Book Your Appointment")
- ✅ Designed with warm editorial color scheme matching existing design
- ✅ Implemented full JavaScript functionality for real-time chat
- ✅ Added quick action buttons for common queries
- ✅ Included typing indicators and smooth animations
- ✅ Proper message formatting with markdown support

### 3. **API Endpoints (app.py)**
- ✅ `POST /api/chatbot/query` - Process chatbot queries
- ✅ `GET /api/chatbot/status` - Check service status
- ✅ `GET /api/chatbot/history` - Retrieve conversation history
- ✅ `POST /api/chatbot/clear` - Clear conversation

### 4. **Documentation**
- ✅ Updated CHATBOT_DOCUMENTATION.md with HuggingFace instructions
- ✅ Updated CHATBOT_SETUP.md with setup guide
- ✅ Added troubleshooting for HuggingFace-specific issues

## 🚀 How to Use

1. **Set HuggingFace Token:**
   ```bash
   # Windows PowerShell
   $env:HF_API_TOKEN="your_token_here"
   
   # Linux/Mac
   export HF_API_TOKEN="your_token_here"
   ```

2. **Install Dependencies:**
   ```bash
   pip install -r requirements.txt
   ```

3. **Run the Application:**
   ```bash
   python app.py
   ```

4. **Test the Chatbot:**
   ```bash
   python test_chatbot.py
   ```

## 🎨 Layout (as requested)
```
LEFT COLUMN:                    RIGHT COLUMN:
┌─────────────────────┐        ┌─────────────────────┐
│ 💬 Medical Assistant│        │ 🔬 AI Symptom       │
│    (Chatbot)        │        │    Analyzer         │
└─────────────────────┘        └─────────────────────┘
┌─────────────────────┐        ┌─────────────────────┐
│ Book Your Appt      │        │ 💊 Medication       │
└─────────────────────┘        │    Reminders        │
┌─────────────────────┐        └─────────────────────┘
│ Upcoming Appts      │        ┌─────────────────────┐
└─────────────────────┘        │ 📋 Upcoming Tasks   │
                               └─────────────────────┘
```

## 🔧 Features
- **Medical AI**: Uses BioMistral-7B for medical queries
- **Fallback Mode**: Template responses if API unavailable
- **Safety**: Input/output validation, escalation system
- **Quick Actions**: Pre-defined buttons for common queries
- **Real-time**: Live chat with typing indicators
- **Responsive**: Mobile-friendly design

The chatbot is now fully functional and ready to use! Tests should pass once the Flask server is running with the HuggingFace token set.

}

    background: var(--color-surface-soft);

    background: var(--color-hairline);
    border-radius: var(--rounded-pill);
}

.chatbot-messages::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    background: var(--color-muted-soft);
}

.chatbot-message {
    display: flex;
    gap: var(--spacing-sm);
    align-items: flex-start;
    animation: messageSlideIn 0.3s ease;
}

@keyframes messageSlideIn {
    from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(10px);
    }
    to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
}

.user-message {
    flex-direction: row-reverse;
}

.message-avatar {
    width: 32px;
    height: 32px;

}

.message-content {
    background: var(--color-surface-soft);
    padding: var(--spacing-sm) var(--spacing-md);

    max-width: 75%;
    font-size: 14px;

}

    background: var(--color-primary);

}

.message-content p {
    margin: 0 0 var(--spacing-xs) 0;
}

.message-content p:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

.message-content ul {
    margin: var(--spacing-xs) 0;
    padding-left: var(--spacing-lg);
}

.message-content li {
    margin: var(--spacing-xxs) 0;
}

.message-content strong {
    font-weight: 600;

}

.user-message .message-content strong {
    color: var(--color-on-primary);
}

.chatbot-quick-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: var(--spacing-xs);
    margin-bottom: var(--spacing-md);
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.quick-action-btn {
    background: var(--color-surface-soft);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-hairline);
    color: var(--color-body);
    padding: var(--spacing-xs) var(--spacing-sm);
    border-radius: var(--rounded-pill);

    font-family: var(--font-body);
}

.quick-action-btn:hover {
    background: var(--color-primary);
    color: var(--color-on-primary);
    border-color: var(--color-primary);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.chatbot-input-area {
    display: flex;
    gap: var(--spacing-xs);
    padding-top: var(--spacing-md);
    border-top: 1px solid var(--color-hairline);
}

.chatbot-input {
    flex: 1;
    padding: var(--spacing-sm) var(--spacing-md);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-hairline);
    border-radius: var(--rounded-lg);
    font-size: 14px;
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    background: var(--color-canvas);
    color: var(--color-body);
    transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.chatbot-input:focus {
    outline: none;
    border-color: var(--color-primary);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(204, 120, 92, 0.1);
}

.chatbot-input::placeholder {
    color: var(--color-muted-soft);
}

.chatbot-send-btn {
    background: var(--color-primary);
    border: none;
    color: var(--color-on-primary);
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    border-radius: var(--rounded-lg);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    transition: all 0.2s ease;
    flex-shrink: 0;
}

.chatbot-send-btn:hover {
    background: var(--color-primary-active);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.chatbot-send-btn:active {
    transform: translateY(0);
}

.chatbot-typing {
    padding: var(--spacing-sm) 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--spacing-sm);
}

.typing-indicator {
    display: flex;
    gap: 4px;
    padding: var(--spacing-sm) var(--spacing-md);
    background: var(--color-surface-soft);
    border-radius: var(--rounded-lg);
    margin-left: 44px;
}

.typing-indicator span {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: var(--rounded-full);
    background: var(--color-muted-soft);
    animation: typingBounce 1.4s infinite;
}

.typing-indicator span:nth-child(2) {
    animation-delay: 0.2s;
}

.typing-indicator span:nth-child(3) {
    animation-delay: 0.4s;
}

@keyframes typingBounce {
    0%, 60%, 100% {
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
    30% {
        transform: translateY(-8px);
    }
}

        max-width: 85%;

        flex-direction: column;

        width: 100%;
    }

488 |         // Format bot message with markdown-like syntax
489 |         function formatMessage(text) {
490 |             // Convert **bold** to <strong>
491 |             text = text.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
492 |             
493 |             // Convert bullet points
494 |             text = text.replace(/^[•\-\*]\s+(.+)$/gm, '<li>$1</li>');
495 |             
496 |             // Wrap lists in <ul>
497 |             text = text.replace(/(<li>.*<\/li>\s*)+/g, '<ul>$&</ul>');
498 |             
499 |             // Convert line breaks
500 |             text = text.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
501 |             
502 |             return text;
503 |         }
504 | 
505 |         // Escape HTML to prevent XSS
506 |         function escapeHtml(text) {
507 |             const div = document.createElement('div');
508 |             div.textContent = text;
509 |             return div.innerHTML;

Successfully redesigned the chatbot UI with major improvements! 🎨

## ✅ UI Improvements Made

### 1. **Removed Trash Icon**
- Removed the clear conversation button from the header
- Cleaned up JavaScript to remove references to the button

### 2. **Distinct Visual Hierarchy**
- **Header Section**: Clean white background with title and subtitle
- **Chat Area**: Beautiful gradient background (#f9f7f4 to #f5f0e8) to distinguish from header
- **Messages**: White message bubbles with subtle shadows for depth
- **Input Area**: White background with clear separation

### 3. **Improved Chat Display**
- **Scrollable Messages**: Fixed height (350px) with custom scrollbar
- **Better Message Bubbles**: Larger avatars (36px), white backgrounds with shadows
- **Smooth Animations**: Messages slide in with fade effect
- **Typing Indicator**: Positioned in chat area with white background

### 4. **Enhanced Styling**
- **Quick Actions**: White buttons with hover effects and shadows
- **Input Field**: Larger, clearer with 2px border and focus states
- **Send Button**: Prominent with shadow and hover lift effect
- **Better Spacing**: More breathing room between elements

### 5. **Layout Reorganization**
- ✅ Chatbot stays on LEFT column (first item)
- ✅ "Book Your Appointment" below chatbot
- ✅ "Upcoming Tasks" moved to RIGHT column (below Symptom Analyzer)

## 🎨 Visual Design
```
┌─────────────────────────────────┐
│ 💬 Medical Assistant            │ ← White header
│ Ask about medications...        │
├─────────────────────────────────┤
│                                 │
│  🤖 [Bot message bubble]        │ ← Gradient background
│                                 │   (#f9f7f4 to #f5f0e8)
│        [User message] 👤        │
│                                 │
│  🤖 [Bot response]              │
│                                 │
├─────────────────────────────────┤
│ [💊 My Meds] [🥗 Diet] [⚠️ All] │ ← Quick actions
├─────────────────────────────────┤
│ [Type your question...] [Send]  │ ← Input area
└─────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 🔧 Technical Fixes
- Fixed JavaScript to work without clear button
- Proper message display with formatMessage() function
- Scrollable chat container with custom scrollbar
- Responsive design for mobile devices

The chatbot now has a professional, clean look with clear visual separation between sections and should properly display all messages!
